**parameter**　interval_unit

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- interval_unit （可選）
- 指定用于計算整個時間配置的太陽輻射值的時間單位。 僅當 use_time_interval 參數設置為 INTERVAL 時，系統才支持此參數。 MINUTE—間隔單位將為分鐘。 此選項僅適用于基于地球的資料。 HOUR—間隔單位將為小時。 DAY—間隔單位將為天。 這是默認設置 WEEK—間隔單位將為周。
- String

**parameter**　sunmap_grid_level

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- sunmap_grid_level （可選）
- 將用于生成用于內部計算的 H3 六邊形格網像元的分辨率。 較低的格網級別值將創建更少的太陽圖區域并減少工具運行時間。 更高的格網級別將創建更小的太陽圖，從而提高結果的精度。 地球的太陽圖格網級別的有效值范圍為 5 到 7。 對于月球，有效值范圍為 4 到 6。 默認情況下，格網級別由輸入表面網格確定。 分析地球表面資料時，如果分析像元大小小于或等于 4 米，則默認格網級別為 6。 如果分析像元大小大于 4 米，則默認格網級別為 5。 對于分析月球表面資料，默認格網級別為 6。
- Long

## 網格太陽輻射 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　4244c16311bd9649c9-54

**english**　Raster Solar Radiation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 太陽輻射工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/raster-solar-radiation.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/raster-solar-radiation.html

**text**　# Name: RasterSolarRadiation_standalone.py
# Description: Calculate solar insolation for the year 2023 at one month
#  time intervals. Return all output radiation rasters.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Run RasterSolarRadiation
out_solar_radiation_raster = arcpy.sa.RasterSolarRadiation(
    in_surface_raster="dsm30m_CA.tif",
    start_date_time="1/1/2023",
    end_date_time="12/31/2023",
    in_analysis_mask=None,
    in_slope_raster=None,
    in_aspect_raster=None,
    out_direct_radiation_raster=r"C:\sapyexamples\output\dsm30_direct_radiation_2023_1mo.crf",
    out_diffuse_radiation_raster=r"C:\sapyexamples\output\dsm30_diffuse_radiation_2023_1mo.crf",
    out_duration_raster=r"C:\sapyexamples\output\dsm30_duration_radiation_2023_1mo.crf",
    time_zone="Pacific_Standard_Time",
    adjust_DST="ADJUSTED_FOR_DST",
    use_time_interval="INTERVAL",
    interval_unit="MONTH",
    interval="1",
    neighborhood_distance="",
    use_adaptive_neighborhood="",
    diffuse_model_type="UNIFORM_SKY",
    diffuse_proportion=0.3,
    transmittivity=0.5,
    analysis_target_device="GPU_THEN_CPU"
)

# Save the output
out_solar_radiation_raster.save(r"C:\sapyexamples\output\dsm30_total_radiation_2023_1mo.crf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/raster-solar-radiation.html#return-value

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

- 太陽輻射工具集

**english**　Raster Solar Radiation

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/raster-solar-radiation.html

**id**　4244c16311bd9649c9-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/raster-solar-radiation.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Raster Solar Radiation，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入表面網格 [in_surface_raster]、起始日期和時間 [start_date_time]、結束日期和時間 [end_date_time]、輸入分析掩膜 （可選） [in_analysis_mask]、輸入坡度網格 （可選） [in_slope_raster]、輸入坡向網格 （可選） [in_aspect_raster]、輸出直接輻射網格 （可選） [out_direct_radiation_raster]、輸出散射輻射網格 （可選） [out_diffuse_radiation_raster]、輸出直接輻射持續時間網格 （可選） [out_duration_raster]、時區 （可選） [time_zone]、按夏令時調整時間 （可選） [adjust_DST]、計算時間間隔的日照 （可選） [use_time_interval]、時間間隔單位 （可選） [interval_unit]、時間間隔 （可選） [interval]、鄰域距離 （可選） [neighborhood_distance]、使用自適應鄰域 （可選） [use_adaptive_neighborhood]、散射模型類型 （可選） [diffuse_model_type]、散射比例 （可選） [diffuse_proportion]、透射率 （可選） [transmittivity]、用于分析的目標設備 （可選） [analysis_target_device]、太陽圖格網級別 （可選） [sunmap_grid_level]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

This parameter is not applicable for analysis on the Moon. Checked—The input time values will be adjusted for daylight saving time. Unchecked—The input time values will not be adjusted for daylight saving time. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "use_time_interval", "label_zh": "計算時間間隔的日照 （可選）", "label_en": "Calculate insolation for time intervals (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether a single total insolation value will be calculated for the entire time configuration or multiple radiation values will be calculated for the specified interval. Checked—Multiple radiation values will be calculated for each time interval over the entire time configuration. Unchecked—A single radiation value will be calculated for the entire time configuration. This is default."}, {"parameter": "interval_unit", "label_zh": "時間間隔單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Time Interval Unit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the time unit that will be used for calculating solar radiation values over the entire time configuration. This parameter is only available when the Calculate insolation for time intervals parameter is checked. Minute—The interval unit will be minutes. This option is only available for Earth-based data. Hour—The interval unit will be hours. Day—The interval unit will be days. This is the default Week—The interval unit will be weeks."}, {"parameter": "interval", "label_zh": "時間間隔 （可選）", "label_en": "Time Interval (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The value of the duration or time between intervals. The default value is dependent on the interval unit specified. The default values for each of the available units are listed below. Minute—60 Hour—4 Day—14 Week—2"}, {"parameter": "neighborhood_distance", "label_zh": "鄰域距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Neighborhood Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance from the target cell center for which the output insolation value will be calculated. It determines the size of the neighborhood. The default value is the input surface raster cell size, resulting in a 3-by-3 neighborhood."}, {"parameter": "use_adaptive_neighborhood", "label_zh": "使用自適應鄰域 （可選）", "label_en": "Use adaptive neighborhood (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether neighborhood distance will vary with landscape changes (adaptive). The maximum distance is determined by the neighborhood distance. The minimum distance is the input raster cell size. Checked—An adaptive neighborhood distance will be used at all locations. Unchecked—A single (fixed) neighborhood distance will be used at all locations. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "diffuse_model_type", "label_zh": "散射模型類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Diffuse Model Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of diffuse radiation model that will be used. Uniform sky—The uniform diffuse model will be used. The incoming diffuse radiation is the same from all sky directions. This is the default. Standard overcast sky—The standard overcast diffuse model will be used. The incoming diffuse radiation flux varies with the zenith angle."}, {"parameter": "diffuse_proportion", "label_zh": "散射比例 （可選）", "label_en": "Diffuse Proportion (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The proportion of global normal radiation flux that is diffuse. Values range from 0 to 1. Set this value according to atmospheric conditions. The default value is 0.3 for generally clear sky conditions."}, {"parameter": "transmittivity", "label_zh": "透射率 （可選）", "label_en": "Transmittivity (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The fraction of radiation that passes through the atmosphere (averaged overall wavelengths). Values range from 0 (no transmission) to 1 (all transmission). The default is 0.5 for a generally clear sky."}, {"parameter": "analysis_target_device", "label_zh": "用于分析的目標設備 （可選）", "label_en": "Target Device for Analysis (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the device that will be used to perform the calculation. GPU then CPU—If a compatible GPU is found, it will be used to perform the calculation; otherwise, the CPU will be used. This is the default. CPU only—The calculation will only be performed on the CPU. GPU only—The calculation will only be performed on the GPU."}, {"parameter": "sunmap_grid_level", "label_zh": "太陽圖格網級別 （可選）", "label_en": "Sun Map Grid Level (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The resolution that will be used to generate the H3 hexagonal grid cells used for internal calculations. A lower grid level value creates fewer, larger sun map areas and decreases tool run time. A higher grid level creates more smaller sun maps improving the accuracy of the result. Valid values of the sun map grid level for Earth range from 5 to 7. For the Moon, the valid value range is from 4 to 6. By default, the grid level is determined by the input surface raster. When analyzing surface data on Earth, if the analysis cell size is less than or equal to 4 meters, the default grid level is 6. If the analysis cell size is greater than 4 meters, the default grid level is 5. For analyzing surface data on the Moon, the default grid level is 6. For surface data on Mars the defaults follow the same logic as for Earth."}, {"parameter": "surface_albedo", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Surface Albedo (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The fraction of incoming solar radiation that is reflected by the surface. Valid values range from 0.1 (no reflection) to 0.6 (high reflection). The default is determined based on the location of the input surface."}, {"parameter": "start_opacity", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Atmospheric Opacity at Start Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The initial opacity value for the atmosphere. It represents the amount of dust and aerosols in the Martian atmosphere that reduce incoming solar radiation. The default value is 0.5, with supported values ranging from 0.1 (clear atmosphere) to 9.0 (severe dust conditions)."}, {"parameter": "end_opacity", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Atmospheric Opacity at End Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The final opacity value for the atmosphere. It represents the amount of dust and aerosols in the Martian atmosphere that reduce incoming solar radiation. The default value is 0.5, with supported values ranging from 0.1 (clear atmosphere) to 9.0 (severe dust conditions)."}, {"parameter": "opacity_values", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Atmospheric Opacity Values (Time, Opacity) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The opacity values for the atmosphere at different times. They represent the amount of dust and aerosols in the Martian atmosphere that reduce incoming solar radiation. Opacity values must be unique and within the start and end date and time range specified for the analysis. Value table columns: Date Time—The specific time for which the opacity value applies. Opacity Value—The opacity value for the atmosphere at the specified time."}]

**signatures**　
- RasterSolarRadiation(in_surface_raster, start_date_time, end_date_time, {in_analysis_mask}, {in_slope_raster}, {in_aspect_raster}, {out_direct_radiation_raster}, {out_diffuse_radiation_raster}, {out_duration_raster}, {time_zone}, {adjust_DST}, {use_time_interval}, {interval_unit}, {interval}, {neighborhood_distance}, {use_adaptive_neighborhood}, {diffuse_model_type}, {diffuse_proportion}, {transmittivity}, {analysis_target_device}, {sunmap_grid_level}, {surface_albedo}, {start_opacity}, {end_opacity}, {opacity_values})
- Pacific_Standard_Time_(Mexico)
- Mountain_Standard_Time_(Mexico)
- Central_Standard_Time_(Mexico)
- Eastern_Standard_Time_(Mexico)

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_surface_raster、start_date_time、end_date_time、in_analysis_mask、in_slope_raster、in_aspect_raster、out_direct_radiation_raster、out_diffuse_radiation_raster、out_duration_raster、time_zone、adjust_DST、use_time_interval、interval_unit、interval、neighborhood_distance、use_adaptive_neighborhood、diffuse_model_type、diffuse_proportion、transmittivity、analysis_target_device、sunmap_grid_level、surface_albedo、start_opacity、end_opacity、opacity_values。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 120255：用于回歸的待預測變量需要五個以上的唯一值。 · 描述

**id**　4255986429e1659ead-1

**english**　120255: The variable to predict for regression requires more than five unique values.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 120201-120300

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/120001-130000/tool-errors-and-warnings-120251-120275-120255.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/120001-130000/tool-errors-and-warnings-120251-120275-120255.html

**text**　要預測的變量必須至少有六個唯一值才能生成預測。

**content_version**　3.7

**content_version**　3.7

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#summary

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-2

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**text**　此工具基于 LAS 點距離地表的高度，使用類代碼值 0 或 1 對 LAS 點進行重新分類。 地表可以來自 LAS 點或單獨的網格圖層。 當使用來自 LAS 點的地面時，需要地面分類 LAS 資料集。 可以使用具有值為 2、8 或 2 和 8 的地面分類點。 美國攝影測量及遙感協會 (ASPRS) 定義了 LAS 格式規范，其中包含類代碼定義，在 1.4 之前的 LAS 版本中，指定 2 表示地面，8 表示模型關鍵點。 以前，模型關鍵點用于識別地面點的子集。 在 LAS 1.4 版本中，模型關鍵點名稱成為可應用于任意類代碼的分類標記，并保留了類代碼值為 8 的定義，以供后續使用。
如果 LAS 資料不具有任何地面分類點，但存在可用的地面點，請使用 分類 LAS 地面點 工具。 如果地面點使用的值不是 2 或 8，請使用 更改 LAS 類代碼 工具相應地重新分類地面點。
使用距離地面的高度梯度對點進行分類可以提供一種有效的方法來對點云進行可視化和過濾，這也有助于進行更為精細的交互式分類過程。 默認情況下，工具對類代碼 3、4 和 5 進行分類，這在 LAS 格式的 ASPRS 規范中表示低、中和高植被。
考慮應用處理范圍來查看指定的 z 范圍值是否適用于 LAS 點的子集。 如果重分類的區域符合要求，即可將其應用于更大的范圍。
當不存在可用的地面分類點，但需要基于高度進行分類時（例如建筑物內部或洞穴的點云），自定義網格表面可能會有所幫助。
標記為保留或重疊的點將被忽略。 保留點最初被定義為不用于分類。 重疊點在處理過程中被忽略，因為包含它們會造成點密度的劇烈波動，并且它們還可能在航線之間引入與噪點相關的校準問題。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#usage

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-6

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**parameter**　height_classification

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 高度分類
- 將用于對 LAS 點進行重分類的類代碼和距離地面的最大高度。 表中各個類的順序可定義將用于處理重分類的 z 值的范圍。 第一個條目的 z 范圍將從地表跨越到指定的距離地面高度值。 后續條目的 z 范圍將從先前條目的上限跨越到其自身的距離地面高度值。 值表列： Class Code—The class code value that will be assigned to LAS points that fall within the range of values derived from the specified height from ground. Height—The order of entry influences the height ranges that will be used to define the reclassification of LAS points.
- Value Table

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-7

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**parameter**　noise

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 噪點分類 （可選）
- 根據點距離地面的臨近性指定是否對點進行重分類和如何進行重分類。 激光雷達資料中的噪點偽影可由傳感器錯誤和空中障礙物（例如，位于激光雷達脈沖路徑上的鳥類）的無意攔截引入。 Low and High Noise—Both low and high noise will be classified. High Noise—Only points that are above the maximum height in the LAS classification table will be reclassified as high noise. Low Noise—Only points below the ground surface will be reclassified as noise. This option is only available when all ground points are used to define the ground surface. None—No points will be reclassified as noise.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#parameters

- 計算統計資料 （可選）
- 指定是否將計算 LAS 資料集引用的 .las 文件的統計資料。 計算統計資料時會為每個 .las 文件提供一個空間索引，從而提高了分析和顯示性能。 統計資料還可通過將 LAS 屬性（如分類代碼和回波信息）顯示限制為 .las 文件中存在的值來提升過濾和符號系統體驗。 選中—Statistics will be computed. This is the default. 未選中—Statistics will not be computed.

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-9

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**parameter**　extent

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 處理范圍 （可選）
- 將評估的資料范圍。 當前顯示范圍 - 范圍基于活動地圖或場景。 繪制范圍 - 范圍將基于在地圖或場景上繪制的矩形。 圖層范圍 - 范圍基于活動地圖圖層。 選擇可用圖層或者使用所有圖層中的資料范圍選項。 每個地圖圖層具有以下選項： 所有圖徵 - 所有圖徵的范圍。 選定圖徵 - 選定圖徵的范圍。 可見圖徵 - 可見圖徵的范圍。 瀏覽 - 范圍將基于資料集。 輸入的交集 - 范圍將為所有輸入的相交范圍。 輸入的并集 - 范圍將為所有輸入的合并范圍。 剪貼板 - 可在剪貼板中復制和粘貼范圍。 復制范圍 - 將范圍座標和座標系復制到剪貼板。 粘貼范圍 - 從剪貼板粘貼范圍和座標系。 如果剪貼板不包含座標系，則范圍將使用地圖的座標系。 重置范圍 - 將范圍重置為默認值。 當手動提供座標時，座標必須為數值，并且位于活動地圖的座標系中。 地圖可能使用與提供的座標不同的顯示單位。 在南緯和西經座標中使用負號。
- Extent

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#parameters

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**parameter**　ground_source

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- ground_source
- 指定將用于確定距離地面高度的地面測量的資源。 GROUND—LAS points designated with the ground classification code value of 2 and model key code value of 8 will be used. MODEL_KEY—Only LAS points designated with the model key classification code value of 8 will be used. SURFACE—The ground height will be based on a raster surface
- String

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-18

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**parameter**　height_classification

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- height_classification [[class_code, height_from_ground],...]
- 將要分配給 LAS 點的類代碼值，這些點位于派生自指定距離地面高度的值范圍內。 條目的順序影響將用于定義 LAS 點的重分類的高度范圍。 第一個條目的 z 范圍將從地表跨越到指定的 height_from_ground 值。 后續條目的 z 范圍將從先前條目的上限跨越到其自身的 height_from_ground 值。 值表列： Class Code—The class code value that will be assigned to LAS points that fall within the range of values derived from the specified height from ground. Height—The order of entry influences the height ranges that will be used to define the reclassification of LAS points.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-19

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**parameter**　noise

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- noise （可選）
- 根據點距離地面的臨近性指定是否對點進行重分類和如何進行重分類。 激光雷達資料中的噪點偽影可由傳感器錯誤和空中障礙物（例如，位于激光雷達脈沖路徑上的鳥類）的無意攔截引入。 ALL_NOISE—Both low and high noise will be classified. HIGH_NOISE—Only points that are above the maximum height in the LAS classification table will be reclassified as high noise. LOW_NOISE—Only points below the ground surface will be reclassified as noise. This option is only available when all ground points are used to define the ground surface. NONE—No points will be reclassified as noise.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-20

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**parameter**　compute_stats

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- compute_stats （可選）
- 指定是否將計算 LAS 資料集引用的 .las 文件的統計資料。 計算統計資料時會為每個 .las 文件提供一個空間索引，從而提高了分析和顯示性能。 統計資料還可通過將 LAS 屬性（如分類代碼和回波信息）顯示限制為 .las 文件中存在的值來提升過濾和符號系統體驗。 COMPUTE_STATS—Statistics will be computed. This is the default. NO_COMPUTE_STATS—Statistics will not be computed.
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

- extent （可選）
- 將評估的資料范圍。 MAXOF - 將使用的所有輸入的最大范圍。 MINOF - 將使用的所有輸入共用的最小范圍。 DISPLAY - 范圍與可見顯示范圍相等。 圖層名稱 - 將使用指定圖層的范圍。 Extent 對象 - 將使用指定對象的范圍。 以空格分隔的座標字符串 - 將使用指定字符串的范圍。 座標按 x-min、y-min、x-max、y-max 的順序表示。

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-30

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name:        Classify Vegetation Points
Description: Classify points representing vegetation with LAS class code values
             of 3, 4, and 5. The code is designed for use as a script tool.
****************************************************************************'''
# Import system modules
import arcpy
import exceptions
import sys
import traceback

# Set Local Variables
inLas = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(0)
recursion = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(1)
lasd = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(2)
extent = arcpy.GetParameter(3)
calcStats = arcpy.GetParameter(4)

try:
    # Run CreateLasDataset
    arcpy.management.CreateLasDataset(inLas, lasd, folder_recursion=recursion)
    # Run ChangeLasClassCodes
    arcpy.ddd.ClassifyLasByHeight(lasd, ground_source='GROUND',
                                  height_classification=[[3, 5],
                                                         [4, 17],
                                                         [5, 120]],
                                  noise='ALL_NOISE', compute_stats=calcStats,
                                  extent=extent)

except arcpy.ExecuteError:
    print(arcpy.GetMessages())

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#derived-output

## 按高度分類 LAS (3D Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具

- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**english**　Classify LAS By Height

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html

**id**　4266d4850b261dbe09-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Classify LAS By Height，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入 LAS 資料集 [in_las_dataset]、地面源 [ground_source]、高度分類 [height_classification]、噪點分類 （可選） [noise]、計算統計資料 （可選） [compute_stats]、處理范圍 （可選） [extent]、處理與范圍相交的整個 LAS 文件 （可選） [process_entire_files]、處理邊界 [boundary]、更新金字塔 （可選） [update_pyramid]、輸入網格表面 （可選） [in_surface]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-by-height.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_las_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入 LAS 資料集", "label_en": "Input LAS Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "LAS Dataset Layer", "description_en": "The LAS dataset that will be processed. Only LAS points with class code values of 0 and 1 will be evaluated."}, {"parameter": "ground_source", "label_zh": "地面源", "label_en": "Ground Source", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the source of ground measurements that will be used to determine height above ground. All Ground Points—LAS points designated with the ground classification code value of 2 and model key code value of 8 will be used. Model Key Points—Only LAS points designated with the model key classification code value of 8 will be used. Raster Surface—The ground height will be based on a raster surface"}, {"parameter": "height_classification", "label_zh": "高度分類", "label_en": "Height Classification", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The class code and maximum height from ground that will be used to reclassify LAS points. The order of the classes in the table will define the range of z-values that will be used to process the reclassification. The z-range of the first entry will span from the ground surface to the specified Height From Ground value. The z-range of subsequent entries will span from the upper limit of the preceding entry to its own Height From Ground value. Value table columns: Class Code—The class code value that will be assigned to LAS points that fall within the range of values derived from the specified height from ground. Height—The order of entry influences the height ranges that will be used to define the reclassification of LAS points."}, {"parameter": "noise", "label_zh": "噪點分類 （可選）", "label_en": "Noise Classification (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether and how points will be reclassified as noise based on their proximity from the ground. Noise artifacts in lidar data can be introduced by sensor errors and the inadvertent interception of aerial obstructions, such as birds, in the path of the lidar pulse. Low and High Noise—Both low and high noise will be classified. High Noise—Only points that are above the maximum height in the LAS classification table will be reclassified as high noise. Low Noise—Only points below the ground surface will be reclassified as noise. This option is only available when all ground points are used to define the ground surface. None—No points will be reclassified as noise."}, {"parameter": "compute_stats", "label_zh": "計算統計資料 （可選）", "label_en": "Compute statistics (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether statistics will be computed for the .las files referenced by the LAS dataset. Computing statistics provides a spatial index for each .las file, which improves analysis and display performance. Statistics also enhance the filtering and symbology experience by limiting the display of LAS attributes, such as classification codes and return information, to values that are present in the .las file. Checked—Statistics will be computed. This is the default. Unchecked—Statistics will not be computed."}, {"parameter": "extent", "label_zh": "處理范圍 （可選）", "label_en": "Processing Extent (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Extent", "description_en": "The extent of the data that will be evaluated. Current Display Extent —The extent will be based on the active map or scene. Draw Extent —The extent will be based on a rectangle drawn on the map or scene. Extent of a Layer —The extent will be based on an active map layer. Choose an available layer or use the Extent of data in all layers option. Each map layer has the following options: All Features —The extent of all features. Selected Features —The extent of the selected features. Visible Features —The extent of visible features. Browse —The extent will be based on a dataset. Intersection of Inputs —The extent will be the intersecting extent of all inputs. Union of Inputs —The extent will be the combined extent of all inputs. Clipboard —The extent can be copied to and from the clipboard. Copy Extent —Copies the extent and coordinate system to the clipboard. Paste Extent —Pastes the extent and coordinate system from the clipboard. If the clipboard does not include a coordinate system, the extent will use the map's coordinate system. Reset Extent —The extent will be reset to the default value. When coordinates are manually provided, the coordinates must be numeric values and in the active map's coordinate system. The map may use different display units than the provided coordinates. Use a negative value sign for south and west coordinates."}, {"parameter": "process_entire_files", "label_zh": "處理與范圍相交的整個 LAS 文件 （可選）", "label_en": "Process entire LAS files that intersect extent (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies how the processing extent will be applied. Checked—All points in the .las files that intersect the processing extent will be evaluated. Unchecked—Only LAS points that are within the processing extent will be evaluated. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "boundary", "label_zh": "處理邊界", "label_en": "Processing Boundary (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A polygon feature that defines the region where LAS ground points will be evaluated."}, {"parameter": "update_pyramid", "label_zh": "更新金字塔 （可選）", "label_en": "Update pyramid (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the LAS dataset pyramid will be updated after the class codes are modified. Checked—The LAS dataset pyramid will be updated. This is the default. Unchecked—The LAS dataset pyramid will not be updated."}, {"parameter": "in_surface", "label_zh": "輸入網格表面 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Raster Surface (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "The raster layer that will provide the source for ground height values. This parameter is only used when the Ground Source parameter is set to Raster Surface."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.3d.ClassifyLasByHeight(in_las_dataset, ground_source, height_classification, {noise}, {compute_stats}, {extent}, {process_entire_files}, {boundary}, {update_pyramid}, {in_surface})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_las_dataset、ground_source、height_classification、noise、compute_stats、extent、process_entire_files、boundary、update_pyramid、in_surface。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。
